# Kecleon, copies through the cap

One step removes one copy of one other card and adds one copy of the named card. The list stays 60. The locked `SET_KECLEON_NAMES` list is not assigned and is not changed. The mirror plays that locked list.

The starting 60 is the lock with one Battle Compressor and one Boss's Orders removed and two Latias ex added. Latias ex says: "Skyliner: Your Basic Pokémon in play have no Retreat Cost. Eon Blade: During your next turn, this Pokémon can't attack."

The cards come in, in this order: Counter Catcher, Penny, Jirachi-EX, Prism Energy, Scoop Up Cyclone. Counter Catcher and Prism Energy are added through 4. Penny and Jirachi-EX stop at 2. Scoop Up Cyclone is added once, after Prism Energy. Basic Energy can fill the deck. The next copy is built on the list that just added a copy. After those counts, that card keeps the count with the highest 0-copy weighted rate, and the next card starts from that count. The list before the card is one of the counts. Every other name still in the list is a cut. The incoming card is not cut while its own count is being built. Nothing is protected. The step keeps the highest 0-copy weighted rate, then the higher worst active foe, then the cut that had more copies, then the name. A dip stays on the path. The second Counter Catcher does not cut the last Counter Gain. That list is measured, and when it is the best cut the swap is left untaken. One Counter Catcher stays, and the next card starts from that list. Penny keeps two copies. Copy 3, cutting Counter Catcher, measured 53.35% and is not kept. Jirachi-EX is added at most twice. The next card starts from two Penny.

Seed 20261116. 400 games per seat. Both seats. Each cell starts a new `random.Random` at that seed and plays its games on that generator. The foe, the seat, and the step are not mixed into the seed. Two lists diverge after the first choice that consumes a different number of draws.

Rules are standard 60. Weights are `max(1e-6, 1 - win rate)` on the starting list, divided by the sum. A tie is not a win, so it sits on the loss side of that formula. The same vector scores every later list. Parked foes keep their 0-copy win rate and are not replayed. Gap standard error is summed on the active set only.

Elapsed 330893s.
